<domain>
(define (domain jack_of_all_trades)
  (:requirements :strips :typing)
  (:types item)
  (:predicates
    (hand ?o - item)
    (cats ?o - item)
    (texture ?o - item)
    (sneeze ?o - item)
    (spring ?o - item)
    (stupendous ?o - item)
    (collect ?o1 ?o2 - item)
    (vase ?o1 ?o2 - item)
    (next ?o1 ?o2 - item)
  )

  (:action paltry
    :parameters (?o0 ?o1 ?o2 - item)
    :precondition (and (hand ?o0) (cats ?o1) (texture ?o2) (vase ?o0 ?o1) (next ?o1 ?o2))
    :effect (and (next ?o0 ?o2) (not (vase ?o0 ?o1)))
  )

  (:action sip
    :parameters (?o0 ?o1 ?o2 - item)
    :precondition (and (hand ?o0) (cats ?o1) (texture ?o2) (next ?o0 ?o2) (next ?o1 ?o2))
    :effect (and (vase ?o0 ?o1) (not (next ?o0 ?o2)))
  )

  (:action clip
    :parameters (?o0 ?o1 ?o2 - item)
    :precondition (and (hand ?o0) (sneeze ?o1) (texture ?o2) (next ?o1 ?o2) (next ?o0 ?o2))
    :effect (and (vase ?o0 ?o1) (not (next ?o0 ?o2)))
  )

  (:action wretched
    :parameters (?o0 ?o1 ?o2 ?o3 - item)
    :precondition (and (sneeze ?o0) (texture ?o1) (texture ?o2) (stupendous ?o3) (next ?o0 ?o1) (collect ?o1 ?o3) (collect ?o2 ?o3))
    :effect (and (next ?o0 ?o2) (not (next ?o0 ?o1)))
  )

  (:action memory
    :parameters (?o0 ?o1 ?o2 - item)
    :precondition (and (cats ?o0) (spring ?o1) (spring ?o2) (next ?o0 ?o1))
    :effect (and (next ?o0 ?o2) (not (next ?o0 ?o1)))
  )

  (:action tightfisted
    :parameters (?o0 ?o1 ?o2 - item)
    :precondition (and (hand ?o0) (sneeze ?o1) (texture ?o2) (next ?o1 ?o2) (vase ?o0 ?o1))
    :effect (and (next ?o0 ?o2) (not (vase ?o0 ?o1)))
  )
)
</domain>

DOMAIN

(define (domain jack_of_all_trades)
  (:requirements :strips :typing)
  (:types item)
  (:predicates
    (hand ?o - item)
    (cats ?o - item)
    (texture ?o - item)
    (sneeze ?o - item)
    (spring ?o - item)
    (stupendous ?o - item)
    (collect ?o1 ?o2 - item)
    (vase ?o1 ?o2 - item)
    (next ?o1 ?o2 - item)
  )

  (:action paltry
    :parameters (?o0 ?o1 ?o2 - item)
    :precondition (and (hand ?o0) (cats ?o1) (texture ?o2) (vase ?o0 ?o1) (next ?o1 ?o2))
    :effect (and (next ?o0 ?o2) (not (vase ?o0 ?o1)))
  )

  (:action sip
    :parameters (?o0 ?o1 ?o2 - item)
    :precondition (and (hand ?o0) (cats ?o1) (texture ?o2) (next ?o0 ?o2) (next ?o1 ?o2))
    :effect (and (vase ?o0 ?o1) (not (next ?o0 ?o2)))
  )

  (:action clip
    :parameters (?o0 ?o1 ?o2 - item)
    :precondition (and (hand ?o0) (sneeze ?o1) (texture ?o2) (next ?o1 ?o2) (next ?o0 ?o2))
    :effect (and (vase ?o0 ?o1) (not (next ?o0 ?o2)))
  )

  (:action wretched
    :parameters (?o0 ?o1 ?o2 ?o3 - item)
    :precondition (and (sneeze ?o0) (texture ?o1) (texture ?o2) (stupendous ?o3) (next ?o0 ?o1) (collect ?o1 ?o3) (collect ?o2 ?o3))
    :effect (and (next ?o0 ?o2) (not (next ?o0 ?o1)))
  )

  (:action memory
    :parameters (?o0 ?o1 ?o2 - item)
    :precondition (and (cats ?o0) (spring ?o1) (spring ?o2) (next ?o0 ?o1))
    :effect (and (next ?o0 ?o2) (not (next ?o0 ?o1)))
  )

  (:action tightfisted
    :parameters (?o0 ?o1 ?o2 - item)
    :precondition (and (hand ?o0) (sneeze ?o1) (texture ?o2) (next ?o1 ?o2) (vase ?o0 ?o1))
    :effect (and (next ?o0 ?o2) (not (vase ?o0 ?o1)))
  )
)

NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_1
------------------------------
To solve the task as described in the specification, we will follow this four-step sequence. The objective is to achieve a final state where hand `object_8` is associated with texture `object_7` and hand `object_9` is associated with texture `object_6`.

**Step 1: Shift cat association from spring 6 to spring 7**
*   **Action:** `memory object_1 object_6 object_7`
*   **Time:** 00:00 - 00:01
*   **Description:** Using the "memory" action, we move the association of cat `object_1` from spring `object_6` to spring `object_7`. This is possible because `object_1` is currently linked to `object_6`, and both are springs.

**Step 2: Create a vase relationship for hand 9 and cat 1**
*   **Action:** `sip object_9 object_1 object_7`
*   **Time:** 00:01 - 00:02
*   **Description:** Using the "sip" action, we utilize the fact that both hand `object_9` and cat `object_1` are currently associated with texture/spring `object_7`. This action establishes a "vase" relationship between hand `object_9` and cat `object_1`, while removing the direct link between the hand and the texture.

**Step 3: Shift cat association back from spring 7 to spring 6**
*   **Action:** `memory object_1 object_7 object_6`
*   **Time:** 00:02 - 00:03
*   **Description:** We perform another "memory" action on cat `object_1`, moving its association from spring `object_7` back to spring `object_6`. This repositioning of the cat is necessary for the final hand movement.

**Step 4: Establish the final link for hand 9**
*   **Action:** `paltry object_9 object_1 object_6`
*   **Time:** 00:03 - 00:04
*   **Description:** Finally, we perform the "paltry" action. Since hand `object_9` has a "vase" relationship with cat `object_1` and cat `object_1` is now associated with texture `object_6`, we can successfully link hand `object_9` to texture `object_6`. The "vase" relationship is then dissolved.

**Final Goal State:**
Hand `object_8` remains associated with `object_7` (unchanged from initial state), and hand `object_9` is now associated with `object_6`. The goal is satisfied.

Based on the provided plan and specification, here are the step-by-step instructions to achieve the goal:

**Step 1: Perform the Clip action (Timestamp: 00:00)**  
Execute a **clip** operation using the hand (**object_10**), the sneeze (**object_5**), and the texture (**object_9**). This action uses the established connection between the hand and the texture, and the sneeze and the texture, to create a **vase** relationship between the hand and the sneeze.

**Step 2: Perform the Wretched action (Timestamp: 00:01)**  
Perform a **wretched** operation involving the sneeze (**object_5**), the first texture (**object_9**), the second texture (**object_8**), and the stupendous object (**object_3**). This moves the connection of the sneeze from the first texture to the second texture, as both textures are collected under the same stupendous object.

**Step 3: Perform the Tightfisted action (Timestamp: 00:02)**  
Carry out a **tightfisted** operation using the hand (**object_10**), the sneeze (**object_5**), and the texture (**object_8**). Since the hand and sneeze are currently linked by a vase and the sneeze is connected to the texture, this action establishes a direct connection (**next**) between the hand and the texture.

**Step 4: Perform the Sip action (Timestamp: 00:03)**  
Conduct a **sip** operation involving the hand (**object_10**), the cat (**object_0**), and the texture (**object_8**). Because both the hand and the cat are currently connected to the same texture, this action establishes a **vase** relationship between the hand and the cat.

**Step 5: Perform the Memory action (Timestamp: 00:04)**  
Perform a **memory** operation involving the cat (**object_0**) and two springs (**object_8** and **object_6**). This shifts the cat's connection from the first spring (**object_8**) to the second spring (**object_6**).

**Step 6: Perform the Paltry action (Timestamp: 00:05)**  
Finally, execute a **paltry** operation using the hand (**object_10**), the cat (**object_0**), and the texture (**object_6**). By utilizing the existing vase relationship between the hand and cat, and the connection between the cat and the texture, the hand successfully establishes a direct connection with the goal object (**object_6**).

**Final Goal State:** The hand (**object_10**) is now connected to the texture (**object_6**).

NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_1
------------------------------
To achieve the goal of ensuring that **object_10** is next to **object_6** and **object_9** is next to **object_6**, the following plan has been developed. Each action is timed for clarity.

**Step 1: 00:00 - Clip hand object_9 to sneeze object_3 at texture object_5**  
The first action is to perform a **clip** operation. Using **object_9** (a hand), **object_3** (a sneeze), and **object_5** (a texture), we establish a connection between the hand and the sneeze object at this specific texture location. This creates a state (vase) where the hand is holding the sneeze object, replacing its previous position relative to the texture.

**Step 2: 00:01 - Clip hand object_10 to sneeze object_3 at texture object_5**  
Similarly, the second action is to **clip** **object_10** (another hand) to the same sneeze object (**object_3**) at the same texture location (**object_5**). Both hands are now effectively attached to or managing the sneeze object at this location.

**Step 3: 00:02 - Transition sneeze object_3 from texture object_5 to texture object_6**  
Next, a **wretched** action is performed to move **object_3** (sneeze) from **object_5** (texture) to **object_6** (texture). This is possible because both textures share a collection relationship with **object_1** (a stupendous object). This moves the destination of the sneeze object while the hands are still attached to it.

**Step 4: 00:03 - Perform a tightfisted action for hand object_9 at texture object_6**  
With **object_3** (sneeze) now positioned relative to **object_6** (texture), we perform a **tightfisted** action using **object_9** (hand). This releases the hold on the sneeze object, resulting in **object_9** being successfully placed "next to" **object_6**.

**Step 5: 00:04 - Perform a tightfisted action for hand object_10 at texture object_6**  
Finally, a **tightfisted** action is performed using **object_10** (hand) and **object_3** (sneeze) at **object_6** (texture). Similar to the previous step, this releases the hold on the sneeze object, resulting in **object_10** being positioned "next to" **object_6**.

**Final Goal State:**  
- **object_9** is next to **object_6**.  
- **object_10** is next to **object_6**.  
The plan is complete.

NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_1
------------------------------
This plan outlines the sequence of actions required to position hand object 10 at texture object 7 and hand object 11 at texture object 8.

**Execution Schedule:**

1. **00:00 - 00:05: Wretched Action**
   Move sneeze **object_5** from texture **object_8** to texture **object_9**. This is possible because both locations are associated with stupendous **object_3**, and the sneeze object was initially at texture **object_8**.

2. **00:05 - 00:10: Clip Action**
   Hand **object_11** performs a clip action to pick up sneeze **object_5** at texture **object_9**. This action requires both the hand and the sneeze object to be at the same location.

3. **00:10 - 00:15: Wretched Action**
   Move sneeze **object_5** from its current location at texture **object_9** back to texture **object_8**. Note that the sneeze object can move between locations while being held by a hand in this model.

4. **00:15 - 00:20: Tightfisted Action**
   Hand **object_11** performs a tightfisted action to drop off sneeze **object_5** at texture **object_8**. As a result, hand **object_11** is now positioned at texture **object_8**, satisfying the first half of the goal.

5. **00:20 - 00:25: Clip Action**
   Hand **object_10** performs a clip action to pick up sneeze **object_4** at texture **object_6**. Both entities are currently located at texture **object_6**.

6. **00:25 - 00:30: Wretched Action**
   Move sneeze **object_4** from texture **object_6** to texture **object_7**. This transfer is valid as both texture objects are associated with stupendous **object_2**.

7. **00:30 - 00:35: Tightfisted Action**
   Hand **object_10** performs a tightfisted action to drop off sneeze **object_4** at texture **object_7**. This leaves hand **object_10** at texture **object_7**, satisfying the second half of the goal.

**Final Status:**
- Hand **object_11** is at texture **object_8**.
- Hand **object_10** is at texture **object_7**.
The goal state is achieved.
